## Summary

This page lists Kubernetes Secrets created, reused, copied, checked, or referenced by checked-in KubeClaw sources.

<!-- BEGIN GENERATED: source-backed reference -->

Generated from: `my-values/setup-secrets.sh`

## Secret Resolution Order

1. reuse complete target Secret unless KUBECLAW_SECRETS_OVERWRITE is true
2. patch missing keys interactively when a target Secret exists and a TTY is available
3. create openclaw-shared-secrets from SOPS file when present
4. copy source namespace Secret from SRC_NS when present
5. prompt or generate values interactively when allowed
6. warn in noninteractive mode when required input is unavailable

## Secret Setup Order

1. setup_shared_secret
2. setup_pipeline_source_attestation_secret
3. setup_redis_secret
4. setup_postgresql_secret when KUBECLAW_DEPLOY_POSTGRESQL is enabled
5. setup_litellm_secret when KUBECLAW_DEPLOY_LITELLM is enabled
6. rewrite_litellm_database_url_namespace when KUBECLAW_DEPLOY_LITELLM is enabled
7. setup_google_sa_key when KUBECLAW_DEPLOY_LITELLM is enabled
8. setup_ghcr_secret
9. setup_git_deploy_key git-deploy-key-nova Nova
10. setup_git_deploy_key git-deploy-key-buster Buster
11. setup_tailscale_oauth_secret

| `key-reference`<br>`pipeline-test-gate-source-attestation` | `<runtime namespace>`<br>the namespace of the rendered Helm release or Kubernetes resource<br>Required when the remote test-gate source-attestation path is enabled. | `publicKey` | Holds the Ed25519 private signing key used by Nova and the public verification key used by Buster for source attestations. | Producer: The secret setup helper generates the key pair with OpenSSL or reuses an existing Secret.<br>Producer evidence: [`my-values/setup-secrets.sh:638`](https://github.com/datrab/kubeclaw/blob/b2fa48a5b332dddbf0ca0b24b202711efd7c768b/my-values/setup-secrets.sh#L638-L638)<br>Consumer contract: Nova reads `privateKey` to sign source attestations; Buster reads `publicKey` to verify them.<br>Consumer evidence: [`my-values/buster-values.yaml:99`](https://github.com/datrab/kubeclaw/blob/b2fa48a5b332dddbf0ca0b24b202711efd7c768b/my-values/buster-values.yaml#L99-L99) key `publicKey`<br>[`my-values/nova-values.yaml:128`](https://github.com/datrab/kubeclaw/blob/b2fa48a5b332dddbf0ca0b24b202711efd7c768b/my-values/nova-values.yaml#L128-L128) key `privateKey` | Owner: pipeline trust owner<br>Publish the new private and public keys together, restart both signer and verifier, run a signed handoff test, and retain no mixed-key state. | Nova cannot sign a source attestation or Buster rejects the attestation signature. | [`my-values/buster-values.yaml:99`](https://github.com/datrab/kubeclaw/blob/b2fa48a5b332dddbf0ca0b24b202711efd7c768b/my-values/buster-values.yaml#L99-L99)<br>`$.extraContainers[0].env[1].valueFrom.secretKeyRef` |
| `reference`<br>`google-sa-key` | `<runtime namespace>`<br>the namespace of the rendered Helm release or Kubernetes resource<br>Required only when an enabled provider uses Google service-account authentication. |  | Supplies the Google service-account JSON file mounted by LiteLLM integrations. | Producer: The secret setup helper imports an operator-selected JSON file or reuses an existing Secret.<br>Producer evidence: [`my-values/setup-secrets.sh:865`](https://github.com/datrab/kubeclaw/blob/b2fa48a5b332dddbf0ca0b24b202711efd7c768b/my-values/setup-secrets.sh#L865-L865)<br>Consumer contract: The LiteLLM deployment mounts `credentials.json` for enabled Google-backed providers.<br>Consumer evidence: [`gitops/platform/litellm/resources.yaml:62`](https://github.com/datrab/kubeclaw/blob/b2fa48a5b332dddbf0ca0b24b202711efd7c768b/gitops/platform/litellm/resources.yaml#L62-L62)<br>[`my-values/infra/litellm-deployment.yaml:81`](https://github.com/datrab/kubeclaw/blob/b2fa48a5b332dddbf0ca0b24b202711efd7c768b/my-values/infra/litellm-deployment.yaml#L81-L81)<br>[`my-values/infra/litellm-values.yaml:4`](https://github.com/datrab/kubeclaw/blob/b2fa48a5b332dddbf0ca0b24b202711efd7c768b/my-values/infra/litellm-values.yaml#L4-L4) | Owner: platform-security<br>Issue a replacement service-account key, update `credentials.json`, restart the consumer, verify provider access, and revoke the old key. | The Google-backed provider rejects requests or the credential file is absent. | [`my-values/infra/litellm-deployment.yaml:81`](https://github.com/datrab/kubeclaw/blob/b2fa48a5b332dddbf0ca0b24b202711efd7c768b/my-values/infra/litellm-deployment.yaml#L81-L81)<br>`$.spec.template.spec.volumes[1].secret.secretName` |
| `reference`<br>`litellm-secrets` | `<runtime namespace>`<br>the namespace of the rendered Helm release or Kubernetes resource<br>Required when LiteLLM is enabled. |  | Supplies the LiteLLM master key and credential-bearing PostgreSQL connection URL. | Producer: The secret setup helper derives or collects the values after PostgreSQL credential resolution.<br>Producer evidence: [`my-values/setup-secrets.sh:805`](https://github.com/datrab/kubeclaw/blob/b2fa48a5b332dddbf0ca0b24b202711efd7c768b/my-values/setup-secrets.sh#L805-L805)<br>Consumer contract: The LiteLLM deployment reads `LITELLM_MASTER_KEY` and `DATABASE_URL` from this Secret.<br>Consumer evidence: [`gitops/platform/litellm/resources.yaml:37`](https://github.com/datrab/kubeclaw/blob/b2fa48a5b332dddbf0ca0b24b202711efd7c768b/gitops/platform/litellm/resources.yaml#L37-L37)<br>[`my-values/infra/litellm-deployment.yaml:41`](https://github.com/datrab/kubeclaw/blob/b2fa48a5b332dddbf0ca0b24b202711efd7c768b/my-values/infra/litellm-deployment.yaml#L41-L41) | Owner: platform-security with database-operations<br>Rotate the master key and database credential under a planned compatibility boundary; update the Secret and verify LiteLLM before accepting the change. | LiteLLM cannot decrypt protected state, authenticate clients, or connect to PostgreSQL. | [`my-values/infra/litellm-deployment.yaml:41`](https://github.com/datrab/kubeclaw/blob/b2fa48a5b332dddbf0ca0b24b202711efd7c768b/my-values/infra/litellm-deployment.yaml#L41-L41)<br>`$.spec.template.spec.containers[0].envFrom[0].secretRef` |

## Examples

Run live checks only in the bound administration shell defined by
[Bind Cluster Authority](../use/install.md#bind-cluster-authority). Keep its
read-only kubeconfig and expected cluster identity in that shell; stop on any
binding mismatch.

Check required app namespace Secrets:

```bash
assert_cluster_binding
: "${KUBECONFIG:?Set KUBECONFIG to the intended cluster binding}"
: "${KUBE_CONTEXT:?Set KUBE_CONTEXT to the intended context}"
: "${NAMESPACE:?Set NAMESPACE to the intended app namespace}"
kubectl --kubeconfig "$KUBECONFIG" --context "$KUBE_CONTEXT" -n "$NAMESPACE" get secret openclaw-shared-secrets redis-secrets ghcr-secret git-deploy-key-nova git-deploy-key-buster
```

Check Tailscale OAuth Secret:

```bash
assert_cluster_binding
: "${KUBECONFIG:?Set KUBECONFIG to the intended cluster binding}"
: "${KUBE_CONTEXT:?Set KUBE_CONTEXT to the intended context}"
kubectl --kubeconfig "$KUBECONFIG" --context "$KUBE_CONTEXT" -n tailscale get secret operator-oauth
```

## Secret Ownership And Failure Signals

| Secret group | Created or reused by | Consumed by | Failure signal |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| shared OpenClaw credentials | `my-values/setup-secrets.sh`; chart values using `openclaw-shared-secrets` | gateway config, agent env, Discord/webhook wiring, provider credentials | missing key warning, gateway auth failure, Discord/provider credential failure |
| Redis | `my-values/setup-secrets.sh`; `my-values/infra/redis-values.yaml` | optional telemetry transport and agent-observability ingestion | Redis auth/connection failure or delayed live telemetry; pipeline and Buster job authority remain independent |
| PostgreSQL and LiteLLM | `my-values/setup-secrets.sh`; LiteLLM infra manifests | optional PostgreSQL chart and LiteLLM deployment | LiteLLM rollout failure, invalid `DATABASE_URL`, missing `LITELLM_MASTER_KEY` |
| GHCR and Git deploy keys | `my-values/setup-secrets.sh`; agent values | image pull and init-container Git clone | `ImagePullBackOff`, missing `/secrets/ssh/id_rsa`, Git clone failure |
| Tailscale OAuth | `my-values/setup-secrets.sh`; Tailscale operator values | official Tailscale Kubernetes Operator | missing `IngressClass/tailscale`, no final-preview URL, operator auth errors |

## Recovery Notes

- Existing Secrets are reused unless `KUBECLAW_SECRETS_OVERWRITE=true`.
- Noninteractive setup never invents provider credentials; pre-create/copy Secrets or run with `KUBECLAW_SECRET_SETUP_MODE=interactive`.
- If a Secret exists but is missing keys, patch only the missing keys or intentionally rerun setup with overwrite.
- Run deployment truth after changing the helper or generated inventory so rendered Secret refs stay in sync.
